### [1] [StateAct: Program State, before Pixels, for Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22798)
*Yan Yang,Xiangru Jian,Ziyang Luo,Zirui Zhao,Yutong Dai,Ziji Shi,Hanshu Yan,Jun Hao Liew,Silvio Savarese,Junnan Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过“状态-接地”（state-grounding）将动作、验证与记忆绑定到程序状态，StateAct在提高成功率与效率方面优于仅靠截图的系统，但将难点从感知转向推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕截图是对底层程序状态的有损渲染，不同状态可能产生相同像素，且许多操作与验证更可靠地通过直接访问程序状态完成，因此应弱化视觉感知依赖，增强状态层面的操作与核验。

Method: 主代理以代码直接访问、修改并读取程序状态；一个专门的GUI子代理仅在少数子目标上执行基于截图的点击交互；独立的完成门（finish gate）基于结构化检查对结果进行验证；通过分派新子代理来管理和保持主代理的上下文聚焦。

Result: 在OSWorld 2.0上，使用Claude Opus 4.8时，StateAct将二元成功率从20.6%提升到26.9%，部分成功率从54.8%提升到61.6%，并实现约9倍更低的每任务成本；完全无GUI子代理的纯代码变体部分成功率为45.9%，低于基于截图的基线。

Conclusion: StateAct通过以代码为中心的多智能体框架实现对程序状态的直接操作与验证，从而提高自动化任务执行的成功率与效率，并将主要失败模式从视觉感知转移为推理错误。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are usually improved by strengthening perception: better models for reading a screenshot and choosing where to click. Yet a screenshot is only a lossy rendering of the underlying program state, e.g., the files, application backends, and DOM that hold the task data. Different states can produce the same pixels, while code can inspect and modify that state directly. StateAct is a code-first, multi-agent harness built around this distinction. Its main agent works directly with program state by using code, while a dedicated GUI subagent handles screenshot-and-click interaction on the few subgoals that need it, just 28 of 108 tasks and 1.1% of main-agent steps. The same direct access to program state also supports verification: an independent finish gate double-checks the saved result for structural failures, e.g., output that is missing, unsaved, or written to the wrong path. To stay on track over hundreds of steps, the main agent hands subgoals to fresh subagents, keeping its own context focused. On OSWorld 2.0, StateAct lifts Claude Opus 4.8 from 20.6% to 26.9% on binary success, and from 54.8% to 61.6% on partial success, at ~ 9x lower cost per task than the same model driven by screenshots alone; a code-only variant with no GUI subagent reaches only 45.9% partial, below that screenshot-based baseline's 54.8%. In general, grounding action, verification, and memory in state, what we call state-grounding, shifts the main bottleneck from perception toward reasoning: failures depend more on what the agent thinks than on what it sees.

</details>


### [2] [The Best Programming Language for Tokenmaxxing: An Investigation of Coding Agent Behavior Across Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22807)
*Zixuan Wu,Carolyn Jane Anderson,Arjun Guha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 不同编程语言会导致显著且一致的 token 消耗差异；对轨迹的结构化执行与文本分析揭示了代理在不熟悉语言上低效的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化不同目标语言对基于 token 的成本影响，理解代理在多语言环境中低效的内在原因，以指导衡量和优化多语言代码代理的资源使用。

Method: 选取五个近期代码生成/修复模型，在难度受控的编程题上对 Python、Java、Rust、OCaml 进行评测；重执行所有中间提交，抽象为测试-结果向量序列并标注跨度工作；对轨迹文本做定性/定量分析（注释、测试偏好、原型语言行为等）。

Result: 在受控难度下评估五个近期模型在 Python、Java、Rust、OCaml 上的性能，发现不同语言在 token 消耗上有显著且一致的差异；通过重执行中间解并将轨迹抽象为一系列测试-结果向量来标注各解之间的工作，发现代理在不熟悉的语言中频繁产生不可编译解、重复修正已通过的解，并在轨迹文本中常以代码注释进行规划、使用自造输入代替给定测试、并用 Python 原型化以规避目标语言的陌生性。

Conclusion: 按语言统计的 token 效率应作为多语言编程代理评测与优化的关键指标；同时可以据此识别最“昂贵”的目标语言并作为优化优先级。

Abstract: Although coding agents are now very effective in a variety of programming languages, this paper first shows that the cost (in tokens) can very significantly by programming language. We evaluate five recent models on programming problems in Python, Java, Rust, and OCaml. We carefully control for problem difficulty, and show that there can be stark variation in token consumption that is consistent across models. To understand why, we analyze both the structure and content of agent trajectories. First, we re-execute every intermediate solution and abstract each trajectory as a sequence of test-outcome vectors, then label the work between successive solutions. This reveals agents repeatedly producing noncompiling solutions in unfamiliar languages and revising solutions that already pass. Second, we analyze trajectory text, finding that agents plan solutions in code comments, distrust the provided tests in favor of inputs they invent, and sidestep unfamiliar target languages by prototyping in Python.
  Our results show that by-language token efficiency is a metric that should be considered when benchmarking and developing multilingual agents, and, for the tokenmaxxer, a guide to the most expensive language to work in.

</details>


### [3] [Do Coverage and Mutation Scores of LLM-Generated Test Suites Correlate with Their Effectiveness? (Replicability Study)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22880)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对多种LLM生成的测试进行大规模复现实验，显示覆盖率和变异得分的指示性取决于评估情景，且先前关于套件规模为主要混杂因子的结论不一定适用于LLM生成的测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验先前关于覆盖率、变异得分与实际缺陷检测之间相关性的结论是否适用于LLM生成的测试套件，鉴于LLM测试生成流程与传统方法存在显著差异。

Method: 大规模复制与比较分析

Result: 发现与先前工作结果显著不同：覆盖率和变异得分的有用性高度依赖场景——在回归式（假设被测代码无已知缺陷）场景中，这些代理指标在模型比较上有意义；而在代码可能已有缺陷且目标是暴露该缺陷的场景中，这些指标不再可靠。此外，测试套件大小并非LLM生成测试中主导的混杂因子。

Conclusion: 建议在评估LLM测试生成时：明确场景（回归对比或缺陷暴露）、优先以真实缺陷检测为金标准、结合多种指标并谨慎解释代理指标；为未来工作提出数据集、统计控制和评估流程的改进方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven growing interest in using LLMs to automate test generation. Prior work commonly evaluates generated test suites using proxy metrics such as code coverage and mutation score. However, studies by Inozemtseva et al. and Papadakis et al. show that, for human-written tests, correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection can largely vanish once test suite size is controlled, raising concerns about the validity of evaluations based on proxy metrics. It also remains unclear whether these conclusions carry over to LLM-generated tests, given that prevailing LLM-based test-generation workflows differ substantially from traditional approaches.
  In this paper, we conduct a large-scale replication study of these two prior works using a wide range of test suites generated by a diverse set of LLMs, and re-examine the relationships among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection effectiveness. Our findings diverge substantially from prior results. We show that the usefulness of coverage and mutation is highly context-dependent: in regression-style settings where the code provided to the LLM can be reasonably assumed bug-free, these metrics can provide meaningful signals when comparing across models; in another common scenario where the code-under-test may already be buggy and the goal is to expose the bug within the code-under-test, they no longer serve as reliable indicators. We also find little evidence that test suite size is a dominant confounder for correlations among coverage, mutation, and real-bug detection for LLM-generated tests. Based on these findings, we discuss how to interpret results from prior studies and provide actionable guidance for evaluating LLM-based test generation.

</details>


### [4] [Evaluating and Mitigating the Misguidance Effect of Buggy Code in LLM-Generated Unit Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22883)
*Junda Zhao,Shurui Zhou,Eldan Cohen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper identifies a "misguidance effect" where prompting LLMs with buggy code leads them to generate unit tests that reinforce bugs rather than expose them, and proposes a specification-based prompting paradigm (using LLM-generated docstrings instead of code) that reduces misguided tests and increases effective, bug-finding tests.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs are promising for automated unit-test generation but prior work shows generated tests degrade when prompts include buggy code. The paper aims to quantify and explain this misguidance and find a robust prompting strategy to avoid it.

Method: Define a quantitative metric for the "misguidance effect"; empirically measure how often tests generated from buggy-code prompts assert incorrect behavior versus find bugs; analyze model internals to show preference shifts; introduce spec-based prompting where LLMs generate a specification docstring to replace code in prompts; evaluate impacts on misguided vs effective tests and on multi-round pipelines.

Result: Empirical results show a severe twofold misguidance: more misguided tests that validate bugs and fewer effective tests that expose bugs. Model-internal analysis confirms preference alignment with buggy behavior. Specification-based prompting significantly reduces misguided tests and increases effective tests, helps multi-round pipelines, and works for both buggy and correct code.

Conclusion: Specification-based prompting (replacing the code under test in prompts with an LLM-generated specification/docstring) mitigates the misguidance effect: it lowers the number of tests that assert buggy behavior and raises the number of effective, bug-finding tests, improving both single-shot and multi-round test generation for buggy and clean code.

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for automating unit test generation, recent studies suggest that the quality of generated tests can be negatively impacted when models are prompted with buggy code. This paper presents a new metric to quantitatively measure the "misguidance effect," a phenomenon where buggy code steers LLMs toward generating tests that validate its erroneous behavior rather than expose it. Our analysis reveals that prompting LLMs with buggy code has a severe, twofold impact: it significantly increases "misguided tests" that assert incorrect behavior while simultaneously suppressing the generation of effective, bug-finding tests. We further corroborate this effect from a model-internal perspective, showing that buggy code skews LLMs' preference toward tests that assert the same erroneous behavior. To counter this, we introduce and validate a specification-based unit test generation paradigm that replaces the code under test in the prompt with an LLM-generated specification docstring. Our results show that this paradigm effectively reduces misguided tests while substantially increasing effective tests, improves multi-round, feedback-driven test generation pipelines, and remains applicable to both buggy and bug-free code. Overall, these results suggest that specification-based prompting is a promising strategy for mitigating misguidance from buggy code in LLM-generated unit tests.

</details>


### [5] [AssumptionMiner: Extracting, Tracing, and Revising Implicit Assumptions in LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22898)
*Jie "JW" Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AssumptionMiner extracts and exposes implicit assumptions from prompts/code, uses AST dependencies for precise localization and targeted regeneration, and demonstrates improved extraction and localization performance on a new benchmark; making assumptions explicit enhances transparency and limited controllability of LLM code generation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world natural-language prompts are often underspecified; LLMs fill gaps with hidden assumptions that can make generated code satisfy tests but violate developer intent. Making assumptions first-class artefacts allows inspection, confirmation, and revision to align generated code with intent.

Method: Introduce AssumptionMiner which (1) extracts an explicit assumption layer (structured inferred constraints/design decisions) alongside code, (2) constructs an AST-based dependency graph to localize and target regeneration, and (3) uses a confidence-weighted ensemble of LLMs to extract assumptions. Also introduces a benchmark of ambiguous tasks with annotated assumptions and a human-verified subset for localization evaluation.

Result: Created a benchmark (180 tasks, 676 assumptions). Assumption extraction: confidence-weighted ensemble achieved F1=0.816, 3.6× improvement over strongest offline baseline. AST-guided localization more precise than keyword and whole-file baselines. Targeted regeneration edits less code than non-targeted approaches but reveals difficulties with cascading edits.

Conclusion: Making implicit assumptions explicit improves transparency and controllability of LLM-based code generation, enabling targeted code updates when assumptions change, though cascading edits remain challenging.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate code from natural-language prompts, yet real-world prompts rarely provide complete specifications. When prompts leave input formats, error handling, or design decisions unspecified, LLMs fill these gaps with implicit assumptions that shape the generated code's behavior and correctness. Because these assumptions remain hidden, generated code may satisfy tests while violating developer intent. We present AssumptionMiner, a framework that makes implicit assumptions a first-class artifact of LLM-based code generation. In addition to code, AssumptionMiner produces an explicit assumption layer, a structured representation of inferred constraints and design decisions that developers can inspect, confirm, or revise. An AST-based dependency graph enables targeted regeneration of only the code affected by a revised assumption. We also introduce a benchmark of 180 ambiguous programming tasks with 676 annotated assumptions, including a human-verified subset for evaluating code localization. We evaluate assumption extraction, code localization, and assumption-guided regeneration. Across open-source LLMs, a confidence-weighted ensemble achieves an F1 score of 0.816 for assumption extraction, improving on the strongest offline baseline by 3.6x. On the human-verified localization benchmark, AST-guided localization identifies more precise code regions than keyword-based and whole-file baselines. During assumption revision, targeted regeneration modifies less code than non-targeted alternatives while exposing challenges in handling cascading edits. These results demonstrate that making assumptions explicit improves the transparency and controllability of LLM-based code generation.

</details>


### [6] [Metamorphic Testing for Clinical ML Models: A Framework Proposal and Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22984)
*Jie JW Wu,Feiyu E,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将变形测试应用于临床预测模型可在不依赖样本级真值的情况下检测与医学常识不符的行为；实验证明其能发现AUROC难以察觉的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用的性能指标（如AUROC）衡量排序性能，但不能保证模型输出与临床因果或医学常识一致；需要不依赖单个样本真实标签的行为正确性检测方法。

Method: 提出并应用变形测试（Metamorphic Testing，MT）到临床预测模型的行为验证流程：构建基于权威临床指南的12条候选变形关系（MR），设计五层验证策略以保证MR的临床合理性，并在公开数据集（MIMIC-III/IV用于设计，UCI Heart Disease用于可行性实验）上评估若干模型的MR违背率与注入故障的检出能力。

Result: 尽管模型在AUROC上表现良好（0.849–0.900），在五条试点MR下仍观测到27%–87%的违背率；注入一个血压特征的符号取反故障时，AUROC未能显著反映该缺陷，而MT违背率提高了31–67个百分点，表明MT能发现标准指标难以捕捉的行为错误。

Conclusion: 变形测试是对传统性能度量的重要补充，能提高临床预测模型的行为可靠性，但需要严格设计和验证MR以确保临床有效性。

Abstract: Machine learning models for clinical prediction tasks, such as in-hospital mortality and sepsis onset, routinely achieve high AUROC scores. However, AUROC measures ranking performance rather than clinical sensibility. A model may rank patients correctly overall while predicting a lower mortality risk when a patient's SOFA score worsens, contradicting established medical knowledge.
  This paper proposes applying metamorphic testing (MT) to clinical machine learning models to evaluate behavioral correctness without requiring ground-truth labels for individual predictions. We design a catalog of 12 candidate metamorphic relations (MRs) for three ICU prediction tasks using the MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, with each MR grounded in an authoritative clinical guideline. We further propose a five-layer validation strategy to ensure that MRs are clinically sound before deployment.
  As a feasibility study, we evaluate the approach on the UCI Heart Disease dataset. Although the three clinical models achieve strong predictive performance (AUROC = 0.849-0.900), they exhibit MT violation rates ranging from 27% to 87% across five pilot MRs. An injected-fault experiment further shows that a sign-negation error in a blood pressure feature remains undetected by AUROC but increases the MT violation rate by 31-67 percentage points. These findings suggest that metamorphic testing provides a valuable complement to conventional performance metrics for assessing the behavioral correctness of clinical prediction models.

</details>


### [7] [Adversarial Test-Hardening for AI-Written Code: An Instrument Autopsy and a Pre-Registered Causal Estimate of the Critic Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23002)
*Jeff Otterson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究用机械判定器考察对抗性的测试硬化循环：一个Tester模型写测试、引入变异缺陷后由Critic模型写能杀死这些残存变异的测试。实验发现早期报告的跨谱系效应源自实现中的截断伪影；在预注册、冻结初始套件的设计下，同谱系Critic能显著提高变异杀伤率（均增量杀伤率0.783，95%区间[0.592,0.935]），跨提供商配置呈正差异但受单次重复影响与工具链不对称性主导。作者发布了协议、收据和分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型自动生成代码与测试，覆盖只能表明执行过的代码而非被验证的行为。研究如何用模型之间的对抗测试循环增强测试有效性，并衡量跨谱系/跨服务比较的可靠性与易受工具链偏差影响的程度。

Method: 使用机械判定（无人工裁判）的对抗测试循环：Tester生成初始测试，注入变异（mutation testing）识别幸存缺陷，Critic针对幸存变异生成杀死它们的测试。实验1揭示工具链伪影并被修正；实验2为预注册、冻结初始套件、五次重复的设计以估计因果增量杀伤率，并计算引导置信区间。

Result: 实验1：对五个Python目标（其中一个单元格无法计分），循环杀死105个单次生成未捕获的变异，且未丢失变异；发现并纠正了输出截断伪影。实验2（预注册、冻结初始套件）：同谱系Critic平均能杀死初始套件留下的78%幸存变异（0.783，95%簇引导区间[0.592,0.935]）；跨提供商配置显示正的速率差（0.178，95%区间[0.039,0.347]），但幅度受单个重复主导且一部分差距源于一个配置的运行失败被记录而非被隐藏。作者公开了所有协议与证据。

Conclusion: 在严格的实验与审查流程下，自动化Tester→Critic循环能有效“硬化”由一次生成遗漏的缺陷；但跨模型/提供商比较容易被运行工具链的非对称失效（如输出截断）污染，比较结果应谨慎解释。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write both code and the tests meant to check it; coverage records what ran, not what was verified. We study an adversarial test-hardening loop under a mechanical oracle: a Tester model writes tests, mutation testing names surviving injected defects, and a Critic model writes tests to kill exactly those, with every verdict decided mechanically, so no model judges another's output. In Experiment 1, on five Python subjects (one same-lineage-loop cell could not be scored), the loop killed 105 mutants that one-shot generation missed and lost none, and the cross-lineage-Critic question returned a pre-declared null. The central finding was an autopsy: an earlier analysis reported a cross-lineage effect at p = 9.5e-66 that was an instrument artifact, an output cap silently truncating the verbose model, caught only by adversarial review of the completed analysis. Review then found a further confound, each arm resampling its own initial suite; Experiment 2 removes it. Under a pre-registered frozen-shared-round-0 design (five replicates on each of four subjects, seeds committed in advance), same-lineage Critic rounds killed 78% of the survivors the frozen initial suite left standing (mean incremental kill rate 0.783, 95% cluster-bootstrap interval [0.592, 0.935]), a within-replicate causal estimate; the cross-provider configuration showed a positive pilot difference (rate gap 0.178, 95% interval [0.039, 0.347]; magnitude dominated by a single replicate) at 5.5x lower arm cost. This compares two named model-provider-harness configurations, not an isolated lineage effect: part of the gap is one configuration's receipted operational failures, including truncation recurrences, now detected and scored rather than laundered. Cross-model comparisons can inherit the asymmetries of the harness that runs them. We release both protocols, all receipts, and the analysis code.

</details>


### [8] [Bifrost: Empowering Pretrained Language Model with Fallibility Representation for Log-Based Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23169)
*Minghua He,Tong Jia,Lingzhe Zhang,Chiming Duan,Xinlong Zhao,Leyi Pan,Cheng Wang,Kangjin Wang,Yinghao Yu,Liping Zhang,Yifan Wu,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Bifrost是一种面向日志多层次故障表征的自监督对比学习方法，显著优于基于自然语言的预训练模型，提升了异常检测、根因定位和故障识别的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言预训练模型的日志表示无法充分表征系统日志中的多层次故障信息（fallibility representations），从而限制了故障诊断性能。需要一种专门面向日志的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出一种受站点可靠性工程师经验启发的日志表示学习方法Bifrost，采用多种自监督对比学习任务以捕捉日志的多层次结构信息（即fallibility representations），通过精心设计的正负样本构造和对比目标来训练模型。

Result: 在三个公开系统和一个工业ML-as-a-Service系统上，Bifrost相比现有PLMs在异常检测F1平均提升9.83%，根因定位HR@k提升18.28%，故障识别Macro-F1提升20.88%。

Conclusion: Bifrost通过针对日志的多层次失败表征设计自监督对比学习策略，显著提升了日志表示在异常检测、根因定位和故障识别任务上的性能，验证了其在公有与工业系统上的有效性。

Abstract: Log-based fault diagnosis is crucial for runtime debugging and maintenance. Existing fault diagnosis methods use language models pre-trained on natural language (PLMs) for log representation. However, system faults are reflected in the multi-level structure of system logs. PLMs pre-trained on natural language struggle to comprehensively capture multi-level fault information, failing to meet the requirements of fault diagnosis. We refer to this information as fallibility representations. To address this problem, we propose a novel log representation learning method, Bifrost. It draws inspiration from the log analysis experience of Site Reliability Engineers and meticulously designs strategies based on self-supervised contrastive learning to learn the fallibility representations of logs. Across three public systems and one industrial ML-as-a-Service system, the log representations produced by Bifrost outperform existing PLMs by average margins of 9.83% in F1 for anomaly detection, 18.28% in HR@k for root cause localization, and 20.88% in Macro-F1 for fault identification.

</details>


### [9] [Backend-Aware Graph Learning for Denoising Outcome Distributions in Quantum Program Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23211)
*Ning Ma,Jun Dai,Heng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出将转译电路作为图并用图变换器+FiLM将后端物理信息与观测结合的Q-BRIDGE，实现了对NISQ噪声观测的高效去噪和更可靠的oracle式测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NISQ后端的噪声导致测量分布被扰动，影响基于oracle的测试判定。需要一种方法将真实带噪观测映射回接近无噪的分布，以提高测试的准确性与鲁棒性。

Method: 使用图变换器对经转译的量子电路进行编码（包含门特性与连通性），将物理后端信息与逻辑结构联合编码；加入基于FiLM的条件层，将图编码与带噪观测结合以生成去噪分布。分别训练针对单个后端的模型和通用跨后端模型并评估性能。

Result: 在23个IBM噪声后端与6类电路族上评估，Q-BRIDGE在去噪性能上大幅优于现有基线。在噪声执行的测试场景中，Q-BRIDGE在检测由bug引起的测试失败方面达到约93.97%–94.90%精确率和82.50%–83.51%召回率，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Q-BRIDGE通过将经编译后的量子电路表示为图并结合物理后端信息与观测数据，实现了对噪声观测的去噪并用于oracle式验证，从而显著提升了噪声感知的量子程序测试可靠性。

Abstract: Testing quantum programs on NISQ (Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum) backends is challenging because the noise disturbs outcome distributions and can affect pass/fail decisions. We present Q-BRIDGE, a graph learning-based approach that converts noisy observations into denoised distributions suitable for oracle-based verification. Q-BRIDGE uses a graph transformer architecture to encode a transpiled quantum circuit, capturing the characteristics of its gates and their connectivity; the physical backend information is encoded together with the logical structure of the circuit. An additional conditioning layer, based on FiLM (Feature-Wise Linear Modulation), takes the encoding as input and integrates noisy observations to produce denoised outcomes. We evaluate Q-BRIDGE on 23 IBM noise backends and 6 circuit families representative of practical workloads. In the first setting, we train a separate Q-BRIDGE model for each backend; in the second setting, we train a single general model shared across all backends. Across both settings, Q-BRIDGE outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline in noise mitigation by a large margin. In testing scenarios with noisy executions, Q-BRIDGE achieves 93.97%-94.90% precision and 82.50%-83.51% recall in detecting bug-induced test failures, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art baseline. These results indicate that considering the graph structure of the transpiled circuits and the physical characteristics of specific quantum backends is a practical route to more reliable noise-aware quantum program testing.

</details>


### [10] [Towards LLM-assisted High-Quality Property Generation for Solidity Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23308)
*Muhammad Wahid,Shahzaib Khan,Mashhood Ali,Muhammad Hassan,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明通过合适提示策略，LLM能够自动生成高质量的Solidity属性；在部分合约上能媲美人工，但整体性能与人工基准仍有差距，且不同合约间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约一经部署不可更改，需在部署前充分测试；属性驱动测试（结合模糊测试）能发现漏洞，但人工编写属性成本高昂。借助LLM自动生成属性有潜力降低成本并扩大覆盖。

Method: 利用多款最先进的LLM（包括Gemini Pro 1.5）通过不同提示策略（零样本、少样本、提示链）自动生成Solidity智能合约的属性；使用变异测试（mutation testing）作为质量度量，将生成属性的mutation score与人工编写的基准比较，并进行逐合约分析。

Result: 在多种配置下，Gemini Pro 1.5结合提示链取得最高平均mutation score 25.99%，接近人工基准31.75%。但存在显著合约级差异：例如LibBit上Gemini在提示链下得到74.34%，几乎等同人工的74.83%。总体表明LLM在某些场景能达到专家水平，但平均仍落后于人工。

Conclusion: LLM可以生成接近专家水准的Solidity属性声明，部分合约上能达到或接近人工编写的效果，但整体性能存在可观的合约级差异。

Abstract: The immutable nature of smart contracts makes it challenging to fix and patch bugs once they are deployed to a blockchain. This implies that security vulnerabilities may be exposed to possible exploitation for a longer period, necessitating comprehensive pre-deployment testing. Property-based testing combined with fuzzing has proven itself as a promising technique for uncovering vulnerabilities. Traditionally, system properties are written by human experts, which is time-consuming and consequently expensive.With the recent advancement in Large Language Models (LLMs) and their ability to 'understand' natural language and code semantics, it may be possible to generate effective properties. This study, leverages state-of-the-art LLMs to generate high-quality properties for Soliditybased smart contracts. We measure the quality of the generated properties using mutation testing. Our results show that LLMs have the potential to generate high-quality properties that are close to those written by human experts. We extensively evaluate LLMs using various prompting techniques (e.g., zero shot, few shot, and prompt chaining). Overall, we find that Gemini Pro 1.5, when combined with prompt chaining, achieves the highest average mutation score of 25.99% among all studied configurations, closely approaching the human written benchmark of 31.75%. However, our per contract analysis reveals notable variance, particularly for the LibBit contract, where Gemini Pro 1.5 under prompt chaining achieves a mutation score of 74.34%, which is on par with human written properties (74.83%). This highlights that while average performance is informative, individual contract level results demonstrate that LLMs can, in some cases, match expert level property generation.

</details>


### [11] [Enhancing Code Understanding for Impact Analysis by Combining Transformers and Program Dependence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23355)
*Yanfu Yan,Nathan Cooper,Kevin Moran,Gabriele Bavota,Denys Poshyvanyk,Steve Rich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Athena: PDG + deep code embeddings → improved, history-free impact analysis; validated on a new 25-project benchmark with significant metric gains over baseline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Impact analysis is cognitively hard; existing coupling metrics are brittle, often require execution or long change histories, and perform poorly on tangled or small benchmarks. A method that leverages code representations and dependence structure but avoids histories/execution could be more robust and widely applicable.

Method: Construct program dependence graphs for source artifacts, derive conceptual coupling using deep representation learning for code (learned embeddings), fuse PDG and conceptual coupling signals into an impact prediction model, and evaluate on a new large-scale benchmark (Alexandria) built from fine-grained bug-fix commits.

Result: On the Alexandria benchmark (25 OSS projects), the best Athena configuration achieves mRR=60.32%, mAP=35.19%, HIT@10=81.48%. Athena outperforms a simpler baseline by 10.34%, 9.55%, and 11.68% respectively, with statistical significance; ablations and qualitative analysis attribute gains to the PDG+conceptual coupling combination.

Conclusion: Athena demonstrates that combining program dependence graphs with deep-learning-based conceptual coupling yields a significant, practical improvement for impact analysis without relying on change histories or execution traces.

Abstract: Impact analysis (IA) is a critical software maintenance task that identifies the effects of a given set of code changes on a larger software project with the intention of avoiding potential adverse effects. IA is a cognitively challenging task that involves reasoning about the abstract relationships between various code constructs. Given its difficulty, researchers have worked to automate IA with approaches that primarily use coupling metrics as a measure of the "connectedness" of different parts of a software project. Many of these coupling metrics rely on static, dynamic, or evolutionary information and are based on heuristics that tend to be brittle, require expensive execution analysis, or large histories of co-changes to accurately estimate impact sets.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel IA approach, called Athena, that combines a software system's dependence graph information with a conceptual coupling approach that uses advances in deep representation learning for code without the need for change histories and execution information. Previous IA benchmarks are small, containing fewer than ten software projects, and suffer from tangled commits, making it difficult to measure accurate results. Therefore, we constructed a large-scale IA benchmark, called Alexandria, from 25 open-source software projects, that utilizes fine-grained commit information from bug fixes. On this new benchmark, our best-performing approach configuration achieves mRR, mAP, and HIT@10 scores of 60.32%, 35.19%, and 81.48%, respectively. Through various ablations and qualitative analyses, we show that Athena's novel combination of program dependence graphs and conceptual coupling information leads it to outperform a simpler baseline by 10.34%, 9.55%, and 11.68% with statistical significance.

</details>


### [12] [On AI Safety and Security Technical Debt in Engineering AI-Enabled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23365)
*Muhammad Tukur,Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo,Tao Chen,Nour Ali,Anis Zarrad,Rick Kazman,Marco Agus,Rami Bahsoon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统综述60篇文献，提出31种AI技术债务并按7类归类，映射到18个信任关切，给出34条缓解指南并提出AITD-MAP框架以支持面向风险的AI工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI在医疗、自动驾驶、金融、教育等高风险领域广泛部署，但其复杂性、快速演进和数据流动性导致新的工程债务形式（AITD），这些债务若未被发现与管理，可能引发维护难题、可靠性下降与安全/隐私风险。基于AI信任风险安全管理原则，研究旨在使AITD可见、可理解并可缓解。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，筛选并分析60篇主要研究，归纳出31种AITD并按根因组织为7类，构建了与18个信任相关关切（6个安全危害、12个安全漏洞）的映射关系，并综合出34条可操作化的缓解指南。最后提出了AITD-MAP框架，将分类、质量/风险影响及缓解策略整合成统一结构。

Result: 产出包括：①31种具体AITD、按7类组织的根因导向分类法；②与18个信任相关问题的映射；③34条针对预防、检测与减少AITD的可操作化指南（8条安全、26条安全性）；④一个名为AITD-MAP的综合框架，支持风险感知的AI工程实践。

Conclusion: 本文认为AI系统的工程债务（AITD）是一个与传统技术债务不同但紧密相关的问题，具有潜在性、跨管道传播性和对可靠性、安全性与可维护性的重要负面影响。研究提出了面向信任（AI TRiSM）维度的重解释，并着重于安全与安全性技术债务的识别与缓解。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains such as healthcare, autonomous driving, finance, and education. While these systems offer powerful data-driven and adaptive capabilities, their complexity, rapid evolution, and dependence on dynamic data pipelines introduce new forms of engineering liability collectively referred to as AI Technical Debts (AITDs). AITDs arise from root causes spanning data governance, model implementation, algorithm design, architectural decisions, operational processes, documentation practices, and testing adequacy. Unlike conventional technical debt, many AITDs are latent and propagate across tightly coupled AI pipelines, leading to maintenance challenges, reliability degradation, and heightened safety or security risks. Guided by the principles of AI Trust, Risk, and Security Management (AI TRiSM), this study reinterprets technical debt through the interconnected dimensions of trustworthiness, focusing on AI safety and security technical debts. We conduct a systematic review of 60 primary studies and identify 31 distinct types of AITD, which are organized into a root-cause-oriented taxonomy comprising seven classes. The analysis examines how these debts map to 18 trust-related concerns, including 6 safety hazards and 12 security vulnerabilities. To support mitigation, the review synthesizes 34 actionable guidelines (8 safety and 26 security) targeting the prevention, detection, and reduction of AITDs across the AI lifecycle. Building on these findings, we introduce AITD-MAP, an integrated framework that connects the AITD taxonomy, quality and risk impacts, and mitigation strategies into a unified structure for risk-aware AI engineering. The framework aims to assist AI software engineers in making AI safety and security technical debts visible, understanding their root causes, and mitigating their presence.

</details>


### [13] [TLA$^{+}$-Bench: An Execution-Grounded Benchmark and Dataset for Natural-Language to TLA+ Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23425)
*Arslan Bisharat,Eric Spencer,Brian Ortiz,Khushboo Bhadauria,Mujtaba Nazari,Beatriz Santos,Anisa Ramos,TaiNing Wang,George K. Thiruvathukal,Konstantin Läufer,Mohammed Abuhamad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出TLA^+-Bench，用完整模型检查作为判定标准，发现“正确率”不是单一数值而是依赖评分选择的区间；大多数模型更会生成可解析但语义不正确的TLA^+，顶级模型正确率低且随难度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动生成TLA^+的评测方式不能保证语义正确性；要用可执行模型检查作为金标准来精确判定生成规范是否满足题设性质。

Method: 收集并标注来自13个公开仓库的TLA^+规范：403个包含可由模型检查器执行的gold规范及897个仅能解析的silver规范；为每个gold规范附带可供模型检查的配置。对若干模型在统一输出集上按不同评分决定（是否要求名称接口、不同评分策略）进行评估，量化正确性包络的上下界。

Result: 数据集覆盖先前工作并额外提供模型写作的自然语言描述、难度与类型标签。实证发现：在固定模型输出下，正确率会随未明确的评分选择在1.7%到18.7%间波动（默认从10.0%到1.7%），表现稳定地显示模型更常写出语法合法但语义不正确的规范；最佳闭源模型默认正确率16%，若给出接口名称上升到26%，开源模型正确率最多1%，且正确率随着题目难度显著下降。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个基于执行判定正确性的基准集TLA^+-Bench，用以弥补现有仅按参考相似度或能否解析来评分的缺陷。通过对可达状态空间的完全模型检查，得出“正确率”并非单一数值而是随评分选择变化的“正确性包络（correctness envelope）”。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly write TLA$^{+}$ formal specifications from natural-language descriptions, but progress is hard to measure: existing resources grade by resemblance to a reference or by whether the output parses, neither of which shows correctness. We present TLA$^{+}$-Bench, a dataset and benchmark that grades by execution. Every gold specification ships a configuration the TLA$^{+}$ model checker runs over the full reachable state space, deciding exactly whether the specification holds the properties that configuration names. The dataset holds 403 model-checked gold and 897 parse-only silver specifications from 13 public repositories, subsumes prior TLA$^{+}$ generation data, and carries four model-written descriptions in two styles from two providers, with difficulty and category labels. Our main finding is about measurement itself: an exact oracle gives not one correctness number but a range. Varying only the grading choices earlier benchmarks leave unstated, on one fixed set of model outputs, the correct rate moves sixfold, from 10.0\% to 1.7\%; adding the interface-supply choice, where the model is told the configuration's names, widens the range to elevenfold, from 18.7\% to 1.7\%. We call this range the correctness envelope and measure each of its bounds. The findings inside it are stable. Every model writes valid TLA$^{+}$ far more often than correct TLA$^{+}$: the strongest is correct 16\% of the time by default and 26\% when given the interface names, open models at most 1\%, and correctness falls sharply with difficulty.

</details>


### [14] [LinkRank: A Learning-to-Rank Framework for One-to-Many Issue-Commit Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23610)
*Abhishek Kumar,Tuhin Mondal,Partha Pratim Das,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 把问题建模为以 issue 为中心的排序＋迭代选择问题，而非独立的成对分类，能更好地恢复一对多的 issue–commit 链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多假设 issue 与 commit 为一对一关系，但实际中常为一对多，忽略这一点会导致不完整的可追溯性恢复。

Method: 提出学习排序框架 LinkRank，对每个 issue 将候选提交作为一个集合来排序并采用迭代的 pick–remove–renormalize 策略选取与 issue 关联的多个提交；在 Unknown-K 情况下使用 ABS 与 REL 两类停止规则；在六个开源 GitHub 项目上评估。

Result: Known-K 情形平均 F1=74.54%（最强基线为48.39%）；Unknown-K 下 LinkRank 在 ABS 规则下 F1=68.84%，在 REL 下 F1=67.02%，均优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 一对多的 issue–commit 可追溯性应以 issue 为中心进行排序并逐步选择关联提交，LinkRank 在多个项目上验证了该思路的有效性。

Abstract: Recovering traceability links between issues and commits is important for software maintenance, debugging, impact analysis, and project understanding. However, most existing approaches assume a one-to-one relationship, where each issue is linked to a single commit. In practice, many issues are resolved through multiple commits, and ignoring this one-to-many nature can lead to incomplete traceability. This paper presents LinkRank, a learning-to-rank framework for recovering one-to-many issue--commit links. Unlike existing methods that mainly judge issue--commit pairs independently, LinkRank considers the set of candidate commits for an issue and identifies the commits that are most likely to contribute to its resolution. To support realistic evaluation, we construct a new dataset from six open-source GitHub repositories. LinkRank follows an iterative pick--remove--renormalize strategy: it selects the highest-ranked commit, removes it from the candidate pool, renormalizes the remaining scores, and repeats the process until the stopping criterion is met. We evaluate LinkRank under two settings: Known-K, where the true number of linked commits is available, and Unknown-K, where the model must infer when to stop selecting commits using ABS and REL stopping rules. Across six projects, LinkRank achieves an average Known-K F1 score of 74.54%, compared with 48.39% for the strongest baseline. In the Unknown-K setting, LinkRank achieves 68.84% F1 with ABS and 67.02% F1 with REL, outperforming the strongest baselines under both automatic stopping rules. Overall, the findings suggest that one-to-many issue--commit traceability is better addressed as an issue-centric ranking and iterative selection problem than as independent pairwise classification.

</details>


### [15] [Where Is the Cost of Third-Party API Routers in Agentic Software Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23624)
*Donghao Fu,Jingxin Li,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过SIDEL框架与400样本数据集实证表明：第三方API路由器的中间人注入能在不被客户端发现的情况下改变代码代理对仓库的实际修改，现有客户端防护不足以保证端到端安全，需供应商层面的输出完整性措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方API路由器作为代理与上游LLM之间的中间层能检查并修改所有请求/响应，造成信任路径被占据；现有客户端权限控制可能在高自治的代码代理场景下失效，但该控制缺口对软件开发任务的实际影响尚无实证测量。

Method: 通过设计四种逐步细化的注入攻击（L1–L4）和实现SIDEL框架（记录、回放、注入与防御评估），使用一个400样本的标准化数据集对四个代表性的代码代理进行端到端实验。评估包括在注入下代理产生的仓库级动作变化、对比白名单执行控制与LLM审查等客户端缓解措施的有效性，并量化防护成功率。

Result: 路由器端注入显著改变代理在代码仓库上的实际操作，且现有客户端侧防护难以检测或阻止这些变化。未经额外缓解时，所有评估代理在所有注入等级下的防护成功率为0%。白名单与被动的LLM审查虽能部分提高抗性，但不能恢复端到端控制，指出需要提供者侧的输出完整性保证。

Conclusion: 路由器端操控是对高自治代码代理的实际威胁：它能导致难以检测的仓库级偏差。客户端缓解（白名单、LLM复核）虽有帮助但不充分，研究呼吁建立提供者侧的输出完整性与更完善的端到端保证；同时SIDEL与数据集为后续攻防研究提供可复现的平台。

Abstract: Third-party API routers have become a common layer that unifies access across increasingly diverse LLM providers. In coding-agent workflows, high-autonomy operation is widely adopted because it reduces interaction overhead. As a result, a third-party API router, which sits between the agent and the upstream provider, inevitably occupies the trusted path. It can inspect and modify every request and response, yet no mechanism verifies alignment between the provider's output and the repository-level actions ultimately executed by the agent. Consequently, client-side permission mechanisms may become ineffective in practice. Whether this control gap produces real, hard-to-detect effects on software development tasks remains empirically unmeasured. In this paper, we conduct an empirical study of router-side injection in coding agents, examining four intervention levels of increasing subtlety: Response Substitution (L1), Response Append (L2), LLM-Polished Injection (L3), and LLM-Polished with Distribution Alignment Injection (L4). Moreover, we develop SIDEL, a framework for trace recording, replay, injection, and defense evaluation, with a curated dataset of 400 samples. We evaluate four representative coding agents, and further evaluate whitelist-based execution control and LLM review. Router-side intervention substantially alters repository-level actions and remains difficult for existing client-side safeguards to detect. Without additional mitigations, all evaluated agents achieved a defense success rate of 0 percent across all injection levels. Client-side mitigations and reactive reviews improve resistance but do not fully restore end-to-end control, motivating provider-side output-integrity guarantees. Our code is available at https://github.com/Riyasushin/SIDE.

</details>


### [16] [Multi-level Code Optimization via Mixture of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23665)
*Yun Peng,Jun Wan,Jiakun Liu,Shuzheng Gao,David Lo,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Optimo通过差分剖析+Mixture-of-Prompts的多级优化策略，有效识别并优化动态语言中的性能瓶颈，在公开基准上显著优于现有基线，带来较大加速与更高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于IR的编译期优化对动态语言支持不足；已有的基于LLM的源码优化缺乏有效的优化目标识别且多为单层次、片段化优化，导致效率提升有限。

Method: 提出基于Mixture-of-Prompts (MoP)的多级LLM代码优化框架Optimo：首先通过差分性能剖析（differential profiling）定位时间关键代码结构作为瓶颈；然后将这些结构路由到若干专门化的优化策略（类似MoE中的专家模型），每个策略以特定的提示(prompt)优化特定代码模式；优化覆盖四个抽象层次，从粗粒度的算法级改进到精细的API使用优化；最后对生成的候选版本进行正确性验证和性能评估，保留满足正确且至少加速10%的优化结果。

Result: 在COFFE与Effibench两个代码效率基准上，Optimo对人类编写代码实现了最高57.48% 的opt%（即正确且至少快10%的程序占比）和最高3.97×的加速；相较最优基线，在opt%上最高提升96.51%；对LLM生成代码，最高获得42.42% opt%和最高13.51×的加速。

Conclusion: Optimo以定位驱动、策略分流和多层次优化为核心，显著提升动态语言代码的运行效率；后续需关注剖析开销、可维护性、泛化性及对更多语言/真实工程的评估。

Abstract: Runtime efficiency is a critical factor that impacts both software quality and user satisfaction. There are many approaches proposed for code optimization to improve runtime efficiency. Traditional code optimization methods operate on intermediate representations (IRs) during compilation for static languages. They are effective but struggle to handle dynamic languages that do not require compilation. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been leveraged to directly optimize source code in dynamic languages. However, these methods fail to identify suitable optimization targets and usually conduct incomprehensive single-level optimization.
  To address these challenges, we propose Optimo, a multi-level LLM-based code optimization approach built on a novel Mixture-of-Prompts (MoP) architecture. In the MoP architecture, Optimo identifies time-critical code structures as performance bottlenecks via differential profiling. These structures are then routed to some optimization strategies, akin to expert models in MoE, each tailored to optimize specific code patterns. Unlike traditional approaches that focus only on statement-level optimizations, Optimo operates at four levels of abstraction, ranging from coarse-grained algorithmic improvements to fine-grained optimizations in API usage. We evaluate Optimo on two code efficiency benchmarks, COFFE and Effibench. Our results demonstrate that Optimo achieves an up to 57.48% opt%, i.e., the percentage of optimized programs that are correct and at least 10% faster than the original programs, and an up to 3.97x speedup when optimizing human-written code, and it consistently outperforms the best baseline by up to 96.51% in terms of opt%. Furthermore, Optimo achieves an up to 42.42% opt% and an up to 13.51x speedup when optimizing LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [17] [Semantic-Enhanced Automatic Refinement of Architecture Recovery Results Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23774)
*Yiran Zhang,Chengwei Liu,Yuqiang Sun,Zhengzi Xu,Weisong Sun,Wenke Li,Wuxia Jin,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Semref通过将LLM语义能力与依赖分析结合，能够显著提升自动架构恢复输出的准确性和可理解性，在小规模真实数据集上展示了明显效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工从实现中提取/修正架构耗时且易错；现有自动恢复工具准确率不足，需大量人工后处理。目标是减少人工干预，提高恢复架构的准确性与可理解性。

Method: 引入了Semref框架：将大语言模型（LLM）的语义理解与程序依赖分析相结合，用于在现有架构恢复工具的输出基础上自动精修实现架构。框架通过LLM对模块语义进行重命名/合并/拆分建议，并用结构性依赖（静态/动态依赖）约束与验证这些建议，从而生成更贴近实现的架构。

Result: 在9个具有公开真值的项目上，针对10个最先进的恢复工具进行了测试，采用5项常用评价指标。Semref在各指标上均有提升，标准化增益范围为17.72%～43.35%；在MoJoFM和a2a_adj上分别获得相对改进118.57%和100.41%。

Conclusion: 该方法有潜力降低架构师的手工修正负担并提升恢复结果质量，但需在可复现性、通用性、消融分析、LLM选择与提示策略、运行效率和实际人工节省等方面补充更多实证与细节。

Abstract: Understanding the architecture is crucial for effectively maintaining and managing large software systems. However, discrepancies often exist between the designed and implemented architectures, which can pose significant risks. To identify these discrepancies, architects need to extract the architecture from the system implementation, which is both time-consuming and error-prone. To simplify this procedure, many automatic architecture recovery techniques have been developed. Yet, their accuracy is often limited. Architects must still invest significant effort in refining recovery results to ensure they accurately reflect the implemented architecture.
  To reduce such manual effort, we introduce Semref, a framework that combines LLMs with dependency analysis to automatically refine architectures recovered by existing architecture recovery tools. By leveraging the LLM's semantic understanding capabilities and integrating structural dependencies, Semref enhances both the accuracy and the comprehension of recovered architectures. To evaluate Semref, we tested on 9 projects with published ground-truth architectures and 10 state-of-the-art architecture recovery tools. 5 commonly used metrics are adopted to evaluate the effectiveness of Semref. The results show that Semref improves accuracy across various metrics, with normalized gains ranges from 17.72\% to 43.35\%. Specifically, for MoJoFM and $a2a_{adj}$ metrics, Semref achieves relative improvements of 118.57\% and 100.41\%, respectively.

</details>


### [18] [The Influence of Fraudulent AI-Generated Responses on Software Engineering Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23805)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Maria Teresa Baldassarre,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过人工与自动检测结合、对比清洗前后数据，发现AI辅助或可疑回复对定量分析影响有限但不无风险，而对定性分析有显著影响，建议在包含开放式回答的调查中采用多重验证措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的普及，担忧其被用于伪造或辅助生成调查回应，从而威胁软件工程实证研究的有效性和可信度。本研究旨在评估此类可疑或AI辅助回复对调查结果的潜在影响。

Method: 对四个软件工程调查数据集进行二次分析，结合人工识别可疑回复、自动化的AI文本检测、描述性统计和主题分析；对比原始数据与经人工清洗后的数据以评估影响。

Result: 过滤可疑回复后，量化结果总体稳定，但若干人口学和分析变量出现中等幅度变化，改变了对特定参与者群体和背景特征的解读；质性结果受影响更大，会改变情境表述、编码重要性和支持解释的证据类型。研究建议采用多重验证方法，尤其在涉及开放式问题时。

Conclusion: AI 辅助或可疑回复对软件工程调查结果的影响取决于分析类型：定量分析通常稳健，但部分人口统计和分析变量会出现中度波动；定性分析更易受影响，尤其在上下文框架、代码显著性和证据性质上改变解释。研究强调在依赖开放式回答的研究中结合多种验证程序的重要性。

Abstract: Background: Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce new concerns regarding fraudulent or AI assisted participation in software engineering surveys. Aims: This study investigates how suspicious or potentially AI assisted responses may affect the validity of software engineering survey findings. Method: We conducted a secondary analysis of four software engineering survey datasets using manual identification of suspicious responses, automated AI generated text detection, descriptive statistical analysis, and thematic analysis. We compared findings obtained from the original and manually cleaned datasets. Results: Quantitative findings generally remained stable after filtering suspicious responses, although some demographic and analytical variables showed moderate variation, affecting the interpretation of specific participant groups and contextual characteristics. In contrast, qualitative findings were more strongly influenced by changes in contextual framing, code prominence, and the nature of the evidence supporting interpretation, shaping how participants' experiences and study contexts were interpreted and characterized. Conclusions: AI assisted participation may influence software engineering survey findings differently depending on the type of analysis being conducted. The findings reinforce the importance of combining multiple validation procedures, particularly in studies relying on open ended responses.

</details>


### [19] [RESTOR: Automated Test Oracle Generation for RESTful APIs via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23963)
*Xun Zhou,Zhen Dong,Mingyu Ren,Qiang Li,JunJie Li,Sifan Wang,Xiaolong Yu,Chaofeng Sha,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Restor通过GRPO微调与数据增强，从单条请求-响应生成鲁棒API断言，在真实工业数据与生产环境中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中常缺乏或已过时的API规范与历史执行日志，测试oracle难以构建；需一种能仅基于单条流量生成高质量断言的自动化方法。

Method: 提出数据增强流水线和基于Group Relative Policy Optimization(GRPO)的轻量级LLM微调流程；通过设计联合奖励函数优化模型选取稳定语义字段、避开动态噪声并生成鲁棒断言；输入为单次观测的请求-响应。

Result: 在246个真实服务的2,300+条API轨迹上验证：关键字段识别F1为85.42%，在语义正确性和鲁棒性上显著优于提示工程基线与通用模型；在字节跳动CI/CD落地后，自动生成测试用例采用率从74.1%升至>96%，降低QA工作量并保证运行稳定。

Conclusion: Restor能从单条请求-响应对生成可执行断言，在黑盒与无规范/无历史日志场景下显著提升REST API测试的断言质量与可用率，并已在生产CI/CD中带来高采用率与QA成本下降。

Abstract: Modern REST API testing faces a critical challenge in defining reliable test oracles, particularly in agile industrial environments where formal specifications (e.g., OpenAPI) are frequently missing or outdated, and historical execution logs are unavailable for newly deployed endpoints. In this paper, we present Restor (Reinforcement Enhanced Single-Traffic Oracle generator for REST APIs), a framework that generates executable test assertions from a single observed request-response pair in a black-box setting. Unlike existing approaches that rely on rule-based templates or massive training logs, Restor utilizes a novel data augmentation pipeline to fine-tune a lightweight Large Language Model (LLM) via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). This training process enables the model to internalize testing "common sense" by optimizing a reward function that jointly encourages: (i) the selection of stable, semantically meaningful fields for validation and the avoidance of dynamic noise (e.g., timestamps or trace IDs); (ii) the generation of robust assertions that withstand logic variations. We evaluate Restor on an industrial dataset comprising over 2,300 API traces across 246 real-world services. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Restor significantly outperforms prompt-engineered baselines and generalist models, achieving a superior $F_1$ score of 85.42% in key field identification and increasing the proportion of semantically accurate assertions. Furthermore, deployment in a production CI/CD workflow at ByteDance confirms its practical value: the system raised the adoption rate of automatically generated test cases from 74.1% to over 96%, substantially reducing manual Quality Assurance (QA) effort while ensuring high execution stability.

</details>


### [20] [Industrial Practice of LLM-Based Test Case Carving and Assertion Generation (Experience Paper)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24000)
*Haozhen You,Zhen Dong,Jingjing Wang,Qiang Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: NL2Test 从自然语言场景描述和对应的执行流量中自动生成可执行的 API 回归测试，结合 LLM 的语义理解与确定性算法做请求切割和断言生成，实测在行业场景上成功率高并已部署生产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统文档不全／过时，QA 常需基于真实流量重构场景，但将原始流量转成可回放且带稳健校验的回归测试既费时又易错。

Method: 两阶段：1) Test case carving：从流量中提取最小可重放请求序列并恢复数据依赖，避免硬编码动态值；2) Assertion generation：根据业务意图生成稳健断言，避开非确定性字段与 LLM 幻觉路径。架构上用 LLM 做语义解析与受限代码合成，用确定性算法做请求过滤、通过值一致性确认依赖、断言路径验证。

Result: 在 51 个工业回归场景上，NL2Test 精确匹配率 82.4%（42/51），容许少量后处理时生成可用草稿率 98.0%（50/51）。生产部署 9 个月内生成 3,196 个测试，代码采纳率 85.4%。

Conclusion: NL2Test 展示了“流量打底 + LLM 语义理解 + 确定性护栏”能显著降低微服务回归测试的人工成本，效果在工业样本和长期生产部署中都很好。

Abstract: Enterprise regression testing for microservice systems is often constrained by incomplete or outdated documentation. In practice, QA engineers frequently rely on real execution traffic to reconstruct business scenarios; however, turning raw traffic into replayable regression tests with stable validation logic remains labor-intensive and error-prone.
  This paper presents NL2Test, an end-to-end approach and tool that generates executable API regression tests from (i) a natural-language scenario description and (ii) a traffic capture recorded while executing the scenario. NL2Test addresses two coupled tasks: test case carving, which extracts a minimal replayable request sequence and reconstructs data dependencies so that dynamic values are bound from their responses rather than hard-coded; and assertion generation, which produces assertions aligned with business intent while avoiding non-deterministic fields and hallucinated paths. To improve reliability, NL2Test uses LLMs for semantic interpretation and constrained code synthesis, and uses deterministic algorithms for request filtering, dependency confirmation via value consistency, and assertion-path validation.
  We evaluate NL2Test on 51 industrial regression scenarios extracted from a large consumer-facing Internet company. NL2Test achieves an exact-match rate of 82.4% (42/51), and produces a functionally usable draft in 98.0% (50/51) of scenarios when allowing minor post-edits. In a 9-month production deployment starting in March 2025, NL2Test generated 3,196 test cases with an overall code adoption rate of 85.4%. These results indicate that traffic-grounded generation with deterministic guardrails can substantially reduce manual effort while improving regression automation in complex microservice environments.

</details>


### [21] [Four Years of GenAI: How Educators and Industry Adapted Their Assessment Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24246)
*May Mahmoud,Nisa Shahid,Izah Sohail,Gulshan Sharma,Hanan Salam,Sarah Nadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过对教育者（56）和招聘人员（24）的两项调查，探讨了生成式AI（GenAI）对入门级软件工程师核心技能和评估方式的影响，发现教育侧以学术诚信为中心，工业界普遍使用GenAI并以策略治理使用方式；双方都倾向于可抗GenAI的实时与高阶任务评估，并一致重视对AI输出的批判性评估、负责任使用工具与自学能力，但在毕业生准备度上存在分歧，招聘方报告的缺口更大，集中在基础性、非GenAI特定技能上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GenAI在软件工程任务中的广泛应用，传统对“入门级软件工程师”所需核心技能与评估方式的假定可能不再适用；研究旨在识别技能与评估的变化、教育与雇主之间的差距以及相应的政策与实践转变。

Method: 通过两项结构化调查收集定量与定性数据：第一项针对56名教育工作者，第二项针对24名招聘专业人士，涵盖不同地理区域，比较他们对GenAI政策、面试与评估偏好、关键技能排序以及对毕业生准备度的看法。

Result: 主要结果包括：教育者以学术诚信为主要GenAI政策考量；工业界近乎普遍采用GenAI并以组织政策规范使用；面试中关于GenAI使用的政策仍在形成中，是否偏好具备GenAI技能的候选人存在分歧；双方趋向使用可抗GenAI的实时互动和高阶任务评估；一致将AI输出的批判性评估、负责任且高效使用GenAI工具及自学适应能力列为最重要技能；但招聘方认为毕业生在这些（尤其是基础非GenAI）技能上准备不足，显示教育与雇主期望不一致。

Conclusion: GenAI改变了技能评估的形式与关注点，但核心应注重培养批判性评估、负责任使用工具与自主学习能力；教育与招聘在认知与准备度上存在脱节，需要在课程与评估设计上更紧密地与工业实践对齐。

Abstract: GenAI's ability to solve a wide range of software engineering tasks is reshaping the software industry, raising the question of what an entry-level software engineer looks like today. In this work, we investigate whether the core skills for entry-level engineers have changed with GenAI and how the assessment of those skills has changed. Through two surveys, we consider both the educational and hiring perspectives, drawing on responses from 56 educators and 24 hiring professionals across diverse geographic regions to identify gaps and misaligned expectations. We find that educators frame their GenAI policies primarily around academic integrity, whereas GenAI use in industry is near-universal, with organizational policy governing how it should be used rather than whether to use it. Policies on GenAI use during interviews are still emerging, and respondents are split on whether they prefer candidates who demonstrate GenAI skills. Both populations are moving toward GenAI-resistant assessments built on observable real-time interactions and higher-order tasks, and both agree on which skills matter most, rating critical evaluation of AI-generated output, responsible and effective use of GenAI tools, and the ability to learn and adapt independently highly. However, they disagree on graduate readiness in these skills: hiring professionals report larger gaps, with the disagreement concentrated on foundational, non-GenAI-specific skills.

</details>


### [22] [Alternative UX Extensions and Their Trade-offs for Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24253)
*Mehdi Elkolei,Omar Abedelkader,Stéphane Ducasse*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文在原有基于AST与惰性生成的语义补全引擎上，提出并实现了四项实用扩展（容错、隐式前缀、分组、骆驼式匹配），并分析了实现与权衡，展示了能在保持模块化与可预测性的同时提升可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有严格前缀匹配对输入错误敏感、框架前缀引发重复输入、补全菜单规模巨大难以扫描、且仅基于前缀无法支持常见的骆驼式缩写，因而需要上诉改进以提高工程师的编码效率与体验。

Method: 基于原有的AST分析与惰性候选生成管线，引入：基于编辑距离/模糊匹配的容错算法；通过前缀规范化或前缀同义扩展实现隐式展开；在候选管线末端或UI层进行分组并改变呈现/排序策略；以及通过大写字母或子序列匹配实现骆驼式缩写支持。系统保持基于过滤器的可组合决策以维持可预测性。

Result: 论文展示了在Complishon（基于AST、惰性候选生成与基于过滤器的候选选择）上新增四项扩展：容错拼写（typo tolerance）、隐式前缀展开（implicit prefix expansion）、分组完成项（grouped completion entries）与骆驼式匹配（camel-case matching）。作者描述了实现细节、备选设计和相关折衷，并证明这些改进可以在不破坏原有模块化架构与可预测行为的前提下集成进系统。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于工程性地将四种用户可感知的补全改进集成到已有引擎中，同时探讨了每种改进的设计选项与折衷，使得系统在精确性、鲁棒性与用户体验之间达到较好平衡。

Abstract: Complishon is Pharo's context-aware code completion engine, built on AST analysis, lazy candidate generation, and filter-based candidate selection. Its existing design already provides strong semantic completion, but several practical limits remain. Strict prefix matching is sensitive to small typing errors, framework prefixes often force redundant input, large completion menus are difficult to scan, and prefix-only matching does not support common camel-case abbreviations. This paper presents four extensions that address these limits: typo tolerance, implicit prefix expansion, grouped completion entries, and camel-case matching. For each extension, we describe the implementation, discuss alternative designs, and explain the trade-offs involved. The main contribution of the paper is to show that these improvements can be integrated into Complishon while preserving its modular architecture and predictable behavior.

</details>


### [23] [Motivations and Barriers to Communicating Software Engineering Research: Insights from Early Career Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24387)
*Shalini Chakraborty,Marvin Wyrich,Sven Apel,Sebastian Baltes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PhD students want to do science communication but are held back by personal and institutional barriers; institutions should provide balanced, context-aware support to enable effective engagement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Science communication is increasingly important for research careers, but little is known about how early-career software engineering researchers perceive and enact it; the study aims to fill this gap.

Method: Semi-structured interviews with 18 international software engineering doctoral candidates; thematic analysis to derive themes across motivations, channels, and barriers.

Result: Participants report strong motivations (collaboration, recognition, impact, advocacy) yet face practical constraints (social anxiety, uncertainty about audiences/venues, limited feedback, insufficient institutional guidance, fragmented platforms). The paper proposes practical implications for tailored support.

Conclusion: PhD students in software engineering are motivated to do science communication but often cannot translate intentions into practice due to personal (e.g., social anxiety), structural (e.g., limited guidance), and contextual (fragmented communication landscape) barriers; tailored support systems are needed.

Abstract: Science communication is increasingly becoming a part of modern research careers, involving researchers to disseminate knowledge, engage broader communities, and increase the societal impact of their work. Despite its growing importance, little is known about how early-career software engineering researchers perceive and navigate science communication in practice. In this paper, we investigate how PhD students in software engineering experience science communication. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 18 doctoral candidates from diverse international backgrounds. Using thematic analysis, we examine three interconnected dimensions: motivations, communication channels, and barriers. Our findings reveal a strong tension between aspiration and practice. Participants were highly motivated to engage in science communication due to opportunities for collaboration, professional recognition, broader impact, and advocacy for themselves and their research. However, translating these motivations into action was frequently constrained by social anxiety, uncertainty regarding appropriate audiences and communication venues, limited feedback mechanisms, insufficient institutional guidance, and challenges associated with navigating an increasingly fragmented communication landscape. Our findings highlight the need for tailored, balanced support systems that empower software engineering PhD students to engage in science communication effectively and confidently across diverse cultural and institutional environments. We outline practical implications that offer initial guidance for addressing these challenges in future work.

</details>


### [24] [Evaluating the Impact of Explainable AI on Trust in AI-Assisted Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24601)
*Zhenhan Gao,Marvin Muñoz Barón,Umm-e Habiba,Daniel Graziotin,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项对34名开发者的组内实验表明：提供详细解释的XAI可提升被试对AI代码审查的主观信任（平均3.99/5），但并不一定提升与AI建议的一致性；中等解释（仅有审查反馈）获得最高的一致率（89.22%）；无解释情况下信任与一致性最低。解释层级未显著影响审查时间，开发者常以可读性与正确性作为决策依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在代码审查领域的广泛应用，开发者难以判断AI建议的有效性与可信度。研究旨在探索XAI（解释性AI）如何影响开发者对AI审查的信任与采纳行为，从而为可信代码审查系统设计提供实证依据。

Method: 采用组内设计，34名参与者对真实代码变更请求进行审查。比较三种基于LLM的审查系统：A（详细解释+审查反馈）、B（仅审查反馈）、C（无解释）。测量信任感、对AI建议的一致性、参与者给出的理由以及完成时间。

Result: 1) 详细解释A的主观信任最高（M=3.99/5），但一致性并非最高；2) B条件（无解释但有审查反馈）的一致性最高（89.22%），暗示过多解释可能促使更多质疑；3) C条件（无解释）信任与一致性最低；4) 解释层级对审查耗时无显著影响；5) 决策理由以可读性与正确性最常见。

Conclusion: 在代码审查场景引入可解释性显著改变开发者对AI的信任感与认同行为：详细解释提高主观信任但可能增加怀疑与质疑，从而降低一致率；适度的信息可能在促成接受方面更有效。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to automate code review, but the reasoning behind their decisions remains hard to understand. Developers struggle to assess the validity of LLM-generated reviews, making it difficult to gauge how much trust to place in them. The role of Explainable AI (XAI) in code review and its impact on trust remain underexplored.
  Objective: We study the influence of XAI on developer trust in AI-assisted code reviews.
  Method: We conducted a within-subjects user study with 34 participants, comparing three LLM-based code review systems with varying levels of XAI support: Condition A (detailed explanation and review feedback), Condition B (review feedback only), and Condition C (no explanations). Participants reviewed real-world code change requests alongside the AI-generated reviews. We measured trust perceptions, agreement with the AI recommendation, the reasoning given for each decision, and the time taken.
  Results: The level of explanation significantly influences both trust and agreement with AI recommendations, but in different ways. Full explanations (A) yield the highest perceived trust (M = 3.99/5) but not the highest agreement, whereas moderate explanations (B) achieve the highest agreement (89.22%). This could suggest that more explanation prompts developers to question AI recommendations more frequently. No explanations (C) results in the lowest trust and agreement. Explanation level did not significantly affect review time. The most commonly cited reasons for decisions were code readability and correctness.
  Conclusion: Incorporating XAI into code review significantly changes trust perceptions and agreement with AI recommendations. These results inform the design and evaluation of trustworthy AI-based code review systems, as well as studies on the human factors of AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [25] [Verification-Conditioned Use: A Qualitative Study on How Generative AI Reshapes Learning, Autonomy, and Market Entry for Junior Software Developers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24606)
*Pedro Henrique Andriotte,Danilo Monteiro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对13位新人访谈的主题分析表明，新人用AI时以能否核验结果为首要判断；AI带来方便但可能造成浅层学习与判断能力流失，持续可持续使用依赖于严格的复核与刻意练习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式AI工具如何从新人自身视角影响软件开发职业早期阶段，包含使用模式、学习、自治性与进入市场等方面。

Method: 对13名实习生与初级开发者进行单独视频访谈；使用Braun和Clarke的六阶段主题分析，采取归纳编码与语义层面分析，归纳出主题并组织成核心概念。

Result: 归纳出16个主题，以“以验证为条件的使用”（verification-conditioned use）为核心：决定使用AI或手工完成工作的主要标准是能否核验结果；出现“自治悖论”（感觉更能做但对结果拥有感下降）和“第一人称否认依赖”；提出理论性贡献“形成性悖论”：AI带来的浅层学习阻碍关键判断能力的形成，而市场对该能力的需求在上升。

Conclusion: 参与者认为可持续使用生成式AI的关键不是工具本身，而是个人的审查实践：在接受AI产出前进行复核、理解后才使用、向工具索要解释，并在非AI辅助环境中保持刻意练习。

Abstract: Objective: to investigate how the use of generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) tools affects the early stages of a career in software development, from the perspective of the newcomers themselves. Method: thirteen interns and junior developers were interviewed individually, by videoconference. Interviews were analyzed using the six phases of Braun and Clarke's thematic analysis, with inductive coding and a semantic approach. Results: sixteen themes emerged, organized around a central concept: verification-conditioned use. Across the study's four research questions (usage patterns, learning, autonomy, and market entry), the criterion that most often decides between AI and manual work is not deadline or task complexity, but the ability to check the result. Two themes expose tensions in newcomers' self-perception: the autonomy paradox (feeling more capable yet less in ownership of the result) and the first-person denial of dependence. Together, these findings point to a theoretical contribution, the formative paradox: the shallow learning that AI induces makes it harder to build the very critical-judgment competence that, according to participants, the market has begun to demand. Conclusion: what makes AI use sustainable, from participants' own point of view, is not the tool itself but the individual practice of reviewing before accepting, refusing to use AI without understanding it, asking the tool for explanations, and keeping deliberate practice outside of AI-assisted work.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [26] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将概念瓶颈融入扩散模型并结合概率正则与梯度掩码，C-VCE 能生成更小、语义可控且视觉保真度更高的反事实图像，无需外部噪声鲁棒分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实解释依赖外部、对噪声鲁棒的分类器，这在噪声图像或像素级编辑下不可靠；通过将可解释的概念层内置于生成模型，避免额外分类器的脆弱性并提高解释可控性和可理解性。

Method: 在扩散模型中嵌入概念瓶颈（concept bottleneck），将分类器作为生成模型的一部分；允许在采样时开关语义概念；加入概率正则项在“改变预测”与“保持原图相似”之间权衡；采用基于梯度的掩码将修改限制到最相关区域。

Result: 在 CelebA 等基准上，C-VCE 在保持或提升翻转率的同时，生成的反事实在视觉上更贴近输入、失真更小；相比依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器的方法更稳定、实用。

Conclusion: 把内部概念层暴露并可控化是一条有前景的路线，可提升强生成模型的可解释性与安全性，特别是在需要可信“what-if”图像的视觉系统中。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [27] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个面向计算节点遥测的基础模型SeT-Diff：通过以传感器语义为条件的扩散生成，实现对时间序列的通用、高鲁棒性建模与多任务零样本能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能计算(HPC)遥测建模方法依赖固定、匿名且位置静态的传感器子集，难以迁移至变化的任务或传感器集；需要一个灵活且通用的节点级数字孪生。

Method: 基于扩散模型的条件生成：对每个传感器的语义描述进行条件化，从而使生成过程与数据集结构解耦，支持重构、插值、预测与虚拟传感等任务。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上，SeT-Diff在重构任务上实现MAE=0.0470；具备零样本传感器置换稳定性（传感器打乱时精度几乎不降）；单一预训练模型可用于数据插补、预测与热场推断，热推断MAE=0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff为HPC系统的数字孪生提供了一种可迁移、任务无关的生成式时间序列建模范式，但需要更多基线比较、细节披露与速度/可扩展性评估以验证其实用性。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [28] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个用于5残基晶格折叠的闭环多代理系统，通过迭代调整哈密顿罚项显著改善VQE优化表现与结构有效性，而不直接暴露真实RMSD给代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测高度依赖哈密顿罚项权重，但现有晶格(格点)工作流通常手动固定这些系数，并且仅在非常短的片段上评估。为了解决罚项选择的自动化与鲁棒性问题，提出一个闭环代理框架来迭代调整基于序列的罚项，从而改善量子变分优化过程的表现。

Method: QFoldAgent由三个核心模块组成：1) 设计代理：给定序列输出条件化罚项系数；2) VQE量子-经典优化管线：在Qiskit Aer噪声模拟下对构建的哈密顿量进行变分量子特征求解；3) 反馈代理：基于能量地形诊断指标与MolProbity几何质量评分，迭代修改罚项。闭环训练中不向代理暴露RMSD，仅在评估阶段使用真实指标。实验在两个数据集（55个带结构片段与100个未见覆盖序列）上进行，报告RMSD、结构有效性、能量改进与Ramachandran统计。

Result: 提出QFoldAgent：一个多代理闭环系统，针对5残基四面体晶格折叠问题，设计代理提出序列条件化罚项，基于VQE的量子-经典管线在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量，反馈代理基于能量地形诊断与MolProbity验证信号在循环中修正罚项。实验显示：在55个QDockBank片段上，中位RMSD从3.64 Å降至3.20 Å；在100个未见序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提至98.7%，回收了87%的初始无效样本，且最强控制器在87%的序列上改进了第3轮能量，同时保持96%的Ramachandran有利构象。

Conclusion: 在五残基量子折叠情形下，迭代代理控制能够系统性地改善优化行为并减少失败案例，但规模与模型简化（晶格、片段长度）限制了结论的推广性。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [29] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs are unstable under meaning-preserving paraphrases—single-prompt accuracy overestimates reliability; generating paraphrases at inference can partially recover latent knowledge and improve robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Although LLMs score well on benchmarks, it's unclear whether their knowledge is robust to paraphrasing; the work aims to quantify reliability across equivalent inputs and probe retrieval inconsistency.

Method: Empirical evaluation across 4 benchmarks and 13 LLMs comparing model outputs on meaning-preserving paraphrases for factual QA and mathematical reasoning; measured instance-level mismatch and answer flip rates; analyzed presence of latent knowledge and proposed a self-paraphrasing inference strategy to recover answers.

Result: Outputs depend on exact wording; overall accuracy changes modestly but instance-level instability is substantial (mismatch rates >23%); conditioned flip rates even higher; models often answer correctly for at least one paraphrase, indicating latent knowledge; self-paraphrasing improves inference performance.

Conclusion: Standard accuracy metrics mask substantial instability; evaluating consistency across paraphrases gives a clearer reliability picture; simple self-paraphrasing is a practical mitigation but not a full solution.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [30] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A structured five-stage workflow leveraging a small medical model and RAG dramatically improves diagnostic accuracy under uncertainty (60.14% vs 23.99%) while remaining lower-cost and auditable.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Demonstrate that diagnostic reasoning needs structured, multi-stage workflows beyond model size and knowledge recall, and that a disciplined pipeline can substantially improve small-model performance in medical diagnosis under uncertainty.

Method: Five-stage harnessing pipeline: structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and auditable final decision; uses JSL Medical Small 7B v2 with RAG; evaluated on DiagnosisArena 915-case benchmark; compared to ablated model and frontier LLMs with cost and latency metrics.

Result: A five-stage pipeline (DeepLens Diagnosis Agent) centered on JSL Medical Small 7B v2 + RAG achieved 60.14% top-1 on DiagnosisArena (915 cases), vs 23.99% for the same model without the workflow; cost USD 0.0072/case, latency 24s, and outperforms Claude Sonnet 4.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro while being cheaper.

Conclusion: Workflow design and disciplined constraints can materially improve diagnostic reasoning, producing inspectable intermediate artifacts and outperforming larger or costlier models; traceability and reproducibility make it suitable for high-stakes settings.

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [31] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI
